- dataviz: Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: "chart", "graph", "plot", "data viz", "visualization", "dashboard", "analytics", "visualize data", "categorical colors", "sequential / diverging palette", "stat tile", "sparkline", "heatmap", "legend", "axis", "tooltip", "chart colors", "color by series".
- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors ("from now on when X", "each time X", "whenever X", "before/after X") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions ("allow X", "add permission", "move permission to"), env vars ("set X=Y"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: "allow npm commands", "add bq permission to global settings", "move permission to user settings", "set DEBUG=true", "when claude stops show X". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.
- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: "rebind ctrl+s", "add a chord shortcut", "change the submit key", "customize keybindings".
- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.
- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.
- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo, defaults to 10m) - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. "check the deploy every 5 minutes", "keep running /babysit-prs"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.
- claude-api: Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK — model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration.
TRIGGER — read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it "looks like a one-liner" — whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Fable, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) — never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens).
SKIP only when another provider is being worked on (overrides all triggers): OpenAI/GPT/Gemini/Llama/Mistral/Cohere/Ollama named in the query; OR `grep -rE 'openai|langchain_openai|google.generativeai|genai|mistralai|cohere|ollama'` over the project hits (run this grep FIRST if no provider named — don't Read the file).
- run: Launch and drive this project's app to see a change working. Use when asked to run, start, or screenshot the app, or to confirm a change works in the real app (not just tests). First looks for a project skill that already covers launching the app; otherwise falls back to built-in patterns per project type (CLI, server, TUI, Electron, browser-driven, library).
- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation
- review: Review a GitHub pull request; for your working diff use /code-review
- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch

2761	  /** Main.hslToRgb 1:1（Main.cs:47266-47290）→ RGB 0..1（s=0 灰阶捷径同源码） */
2762	  private static glowHslToRgb(hue: number, sat: number, lum: number): [number, number, number] {
2763	    if (sat === 0) return [lum, lum, lum];
2764	    const v2 = lum < 0.5 ? lum * (1 + sat) : lum + sat - lum * sat;
2765	    const v1 = 2 * lum - v2;
2766	    const h2r = (h: number): number => {
2767	      if (h < 0) h += 1;
2768	      if (h > 1) h -= 1;
2769	      if (6 * h < 1) return v1 + (v2 - v1) * 6 * h;
2770	      if (2 * h < 1) return v2;
2771	      if (3 * h < 1) return v1 + (v2 - v1) * (2 / 3 - h) * 6;
2772	      return v1;
2773	    };
2774	    return [h2r(hue + 1 / 3), h2r(hue), h2r(hue - 1 / 3)];
2775	  }

2830	  private drawNpcGlow(
2831	    e: Enemy,
2832	    r: { img: CanvasImageSource; sx: number; sy: number; sw: number; sh: number },
2833	    bx: number, by: number,
2834	  ) {
2835	    if (!this.atlas) return;

2839	    if (!img) return;

2846	    const gh = Math.max(1, Math.floor(img.height / frames));
2847	    const row = Math.min(frames - 1, Math.max(0, Math.floor(r.sy / Math.max(1, r.sh))));
2848	    const sy = Math.min(img.height - gh, row * gh);
2849	    ctx.save();
2850	    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
2851	    // 第三批通用 alpha 表达式（g.a）：数字=常数 / '255a'=(255-α)/255 / '128a2'=(128-α/2)/255
2852	    // / 'opacity'=GetAlpha(White)。缺省（旧两批）走下方 id 链。
2853	    const aExpr = g.a;
2854	    const resolveAlpha = (): number => {
2855	      if (aExpr === undefined) return -1; // 哨兵：走旧链
2856	      if (typeof aExpr === 'number') return aExpr;
2857	      if (aExpr === '255a') return (255 - alphaRaw) / 255;
2858	      if (aExpr === '128a2') return (128 - alphaRaw / 2) / 255;
2859	      return opacity;
2860	    };

2864	      // k + 光照项（恒 k），或 UnitX.RotatedBy 固定 px :25736/:25746）
2865	      const offX = (g.dx ?? 0) / scale;   // 518 halfSize+(-10,0) → 图像右移 10 世界 px
2866	      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, resolveAlpha()));
2867	      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, sy, img.width, gh, bx + offX, by, r.sw, r.sh);
2868	      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (64 / 255) * opacity));

2871	      const svx = e.vanillaId === 416 && e.master && !e.master.dead ? e.master.vx : e.vx;
2872	      const svy = e.vanillaId === 416 && e.master && !e.master.dead ? e.master.vy : e.vy;
2873	      const fixed = g.fixed4;
2874	      const mult = g.velMult ?? 0.5;
2875	      for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
2876	        const ang = k * Math.PI / 2;
2877	        const dx = (fixed !== undefined
2878	          ? Math.cos(ang) * fixed                       // UnitX.RotatedBy(k*π/2)×n
2879	          : (svx * Math.cos(ang) - svy * Math.sin(ang)) * mult) / scale;
2880	        const dy = (fixed !== undefined
2881	          ? Math.sin(ang) * fixed
2882	          : (svx * Math.sin(ang) + svy * Math.cos(ang)) * mult) / scale;
2883	        ctx.drawImage(img, 0, sy, img.width, gh, bx + offX + dx, by + dy, r.sw, r.sh);
2884	      }
2885	    } else if (g.mode === 'orbit') {
2886	      // 环绕轨道族（Main.cs:26086-26128）：碰撞盒中心 1 份 + 极角 i/count×2π+phase
2887	      // 环绕 count 份拷贝。锚点 = Center（:26091/:26107/:26123）——与 frame 族
2888	      // Bottom+4 不同源，故不沿用 bx/by，按 noGravity 自行换算盒心。
2889	      const prm = Renderer.npcGlowOrbitParams(
2890	        e.vanillaId ?? -1, performance.now() / 1000, e.ai2,
2891	        (e.vanillaId === 568 || e.vanillaId === 569) ? 60 : e.lai3,
2892	      );
2893	      if (prm) {

function fireWalkerProj(
  game: GameHooks, e: Enemy, p: Walker107P, tx: number, ty: number,
): void {
  const mx = e.cx + p.muzzleDx * e.facing;
  const my = e.cy + (p.muzzleAtBottom ? e.h / 2 - 1 : p.muzzleDy);
  let vx = tx - mx, vy = ty - my;
  vy -= Math.abs(vx) * p.arc;              // 弧线补偿
  const len = Math.hypot(vx, vy) || 1;
  vx = (vx / len) * p.speed;
  vy = (vy / len) * p.speed;
  const sx = mx + vx * p.spawnOffset, sy = my + vy * p.spawnOffset;

  clearMoonlordDrama(): void {

    this.dramaWhitening = 0;
    this.dramaRequestedLight = 0;

  /** 每帧扫描 398（Renderer frameCtx.entities）→ 死亡戏剧触发/爆炸/白光请求 */
  private updateMoonlordDrama(dtFrames: number): void {
    const fc = this.frameCtx;
    const world = fc?.world ?? null;
    let core: { cx: number; cy: number; ai0: number; ai1: number } | null = null;
    if (fc) {
      for (const e of fc.entities) {
        if (e.vanillaId !== 398 || e.dead) continue;
        core = { cx: e.cx, cy: e.cy, ai0: e.ai0 ?? 0, ai1: e.ai1 ?? 0 };
        break;
      }
    }
    const prev = this.dramaCore;
    this.dramaCore = core;
    this.dramaLightRequestedThisFrame = false;

    if (core) {
      // 死亡演出段（ai0==2）：每 3t 一簇爆炎（:36981-36998，落点须界内非实心）
      if (core.ai0 === 2 && core.ai1 % 3 === 0 && core.ai1 > 60 && core.ai1 < 580) {
        const ang = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
        const rad = 20 + Math.random() * 400;
        const ex = core.cx + Math.cos(ang) * rad, ey = core.cy + Math.sin(ang) * rad;
        const tx = Math.floor(ex / 16), ty = Math.floor(ey / 16);
        const ok = world ? (world.store.inBounds(tx, ty) && !world.store.isSolid(tx, ty)) : true;
        if (ok) this.addMoonlordExplosion(ex, ey);
      }
      // 白光请求：出生态 -1/-2（ai1<60 → ai1/30）与死亡态尾段（≥480 → (ai1-480)/120）
      if ((core.ai0 === -1 || core.ai0 === -2) && core.ai1 < 60) {
        this.requestDramaLight(core.ai1 / 30, core.cx, core.cy);   // :36796/:36820
      } else if (core.ai0 === 2 && core.ai1 >= 480) {
        this.requestDramaLight((core.ai1 - 480) / 120, core.cx, core.cy);  // :37055
      }
      // 终死（ai1≥600 → life=0 + HitEffect(0,1337) → ThrowPieces）：掷点跨线或实体消失兜底
      const diedNow = prev !== null && prev.ai1 < 600 && core.ai1 >= 600 && core.ai0 === 2;
      const vanished = prev !== null && prev.ai0 === 2 && prev.ai1 >= 590;
      if (diedNow || vanished) {
        this.throwMoonlordPieces(vanished && !diedNow ? prev.cx : core.cx,
          vanished && !diedNow ? prev.cy : core.cy, Math.floor(Math.random() * 100));
      }
    }
    // Update :158-196：碎片/爆炸推进 + 白光 0.02/帧 逼近（无光源请求 → 目标 0）
    for (const p of this.dramaPieces) p.update(world?.w ?? 0, world?.h ?? 0);
    this.dramaPieces = this.dramaPieces.filter((p) => !p.dead);
    for (const ex of this.dramaExplosions) ex.update();
    this.dramaExplosions = this.dramaExplosions.filter((ex) => !ex.dead);
    if (!this.dramaLightRequestedThisFrame) this.dramaRequestedLight = 0;   // :190-193
    const target = this.dramaRequestedLight;
    this.dramaWhitening += Math.max(-0.02, Math.min(0.02, target - this.dramaWhitening)) * dtFrames;
    this.dramaWhitening = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, this.dramaWhitening));
  }

  /** ThrowPieces（:231-242）：四件骨骼碎块自核心飞散（UnitY 旋 NextFloat()×π/2−π/4+π
   *  的 6/10/8/12 速；角速度 ±0.05）。种子 = Main.rand.Next(100)（NPC.cs:83496 调用点） */
  private throwMoonlordPieces(cx: number, cy: number, seed: number): void {
    if (this.dramaPieces.length > 0) return;   // 一次死亡一组
    const r = seededRandom(seed);
    const defs: Array<[DramaPieceKind, number, number, number, number, number]> = [
      // [件, originX, originY, 距核心偏移x, 偏移y, 速度]
      ['spine', 64, 150, 0, 50, 6],        // :235
      ['shoulder', 40, 120, 50, -120, 10], // :237
      ['torso', 192, 252, 0, 0, 8],        // :239
      ['head', 138, 185, 0, -200, 12],     // :241
    ];
    for (const [kind, ox, oy, dx, dy, spd] of defs) {
      const ang = r() * (Math.PI / 2) - Math.PI / 4 + Math.PI;
      this.dramaPieces.push(new DramaPiece(kind, cx + dx, cy + dy,
        Math.cos(ang) * spd, Math.sin(ang) * spd, ox, oy,
        r() * 0.1 - 0.05));
    }
  }

  /** AddExplosion（:244-247）：7 帧爆炎，帧速 Next(2,4) */
  addMoonlordExplosion(x: number, y: number): void {
    this.dramaExplosions.push(new DramaExplosion(x, y, 2 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 2)));
  }

  /** RequestLight（:249-260）：本帧光源 + 目标取 max（钳 1） */
  private requestDramaLight(light: number, x: number, y: number): void {
    const player = this.frameCtx?.player;
    // :181-188 光源须距 metrics.Center <2000 才生效
    if (player && Math.hypot(player.cx - x, player.cy - y) >= 2000) return;
    this.dramaLightRequestedThisFrame = true;
    const v = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, light));
    if (v > this.dramaRequestedLight) this.dramaRequestedLight = v;
  }

  /** DrawPieces/DrawExplosions（:198-220）：世界位 - screenPosition；原版画在 tile 层
   *  之前（Main.cs:62711-62712，WoF/背景碎块之后）——本仓在 pre-world 变换段调用 */
  private drawMoonlordDrama(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, camX: number, camY: number,
    viewW: number, viewH: number, z: number): void {
    this.updateMoonlordDrama(1);
    if (!this.dramaPieces.length && !this.dramaExplosions.length) return;
    const screenX = camX - viewW / 2, screenY = camY - viewH / 2;
    const sx = (wx: number) => (wx - screenX) * z;
    const sy = (wy: number) => (wy - screenY) * z;
    // 屏中心 ±(视口+1000) 剔除（:200/:212）
    ctx.save();
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    for (const p of this.dramaPieces) {
      const px = sx(p.x), py = sy(p.y);
      if (px < -1000 || px > viewW + 1000 || py < -1000 || py > viewH + 1000) continue;
      const tex = dramaTex(p.kind);
      if (!tex || !tex.complete || !tex.naturalWidth) continue;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      ctx.translate(px, py);
      ctx.rotate(p.rot);
      ctx.drawImage(tex, -p.ox, -p.oy);
      ctx.restore();
    }
    for (const ex of this.dramaExplosions) {
      const px = sx(ex.x), py = sy(ex.y);
      if (px < -1000 || px > viewW + 1000 || py < -1000 || py > viewH + 1000) continue;
      const tex = dramaTex('explosion');
      if (!tex || !tex.complete || !tex.naturalWidth) continue;
      const fw = tex.naturalWidth, fh = tex.naturalHeight / 7;   // Frame(1,7)（:121）
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 127 / 255;                                // GetLight :142-145 恒 (255,255,255,127)
      ctx.drawImage(tex, 0, Math.min(6, Math.floor(ex.counter / ex.speed)) * fh, fw, fh,
        px - fw / 2, py - fh / 2, fw, fh);
      ctx.restore();
    }
    ctx.restore();
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  }

  /** DrawWhite（:222-229）：全屏白 × whitening。原版画在 ScreenDarkness.DrawFront 之后
   *  （Main.cs:61763，几乎最末）；本仓在世界变换段以恒等变换近似（登记：位置偏早） */
  private drawMoonlordWhitening(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, viewW: number, viewH: number): void {
    if (this.dramaWhitening <= 0.001) return;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
    ctx.globalAlpha = Math.min(1, this.dramaWhitening);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, viewW, viewH);
    ctx.restore();
  }

  // ================= 晨昏耀斑（NextHorizonRenderer Sunflare 族 1:1） =================

  /** 涟漪绘制（世界坐标，画在 tile/液体层之上——Renderer 世界变换段调用） */
  private drawRipples(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D): void {
    const w = this.weatherRef;
    if (!w || w.cloudAlpha <= 0 || !w.ripples.length) return;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    for (const rp of w.ripples) {
      const k = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, rp.t / rp.life));
      const r = 4 + 20 * k;                                   // 扩散半径 4→24px
      const a = (1 - k) * 0.32 * Math.max(0.2, Math.min(1, w.cloudAlpha));
      if (a <= 0.01) continue;
      ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(225,238,255,${a.toFixed(3)})`;
      ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.ellipse(rp.x, rp.y, r, r * 0.42, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.stroke();
      if (k < 0.6) {                                          // 内环只在前段
        ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(225,238,255,${(a * 0.6).toFixed(3)})`;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(rp.x, rp.y, r * 0.5, r * 0.21, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.stroke();
      }
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }

  /** 世界变换段绘制（Renderer.render 2d' 区一行调用）：涟漪（世界坐标）+

    if (this.sunScreen) {

        this.atmoValue(camY), this.weatherRef?.cloudAlpha ?? 0);
      this.drawLensFlare(ctx, viewW, viewH, v.sunset, v.sunrise, v.celestial);
    }
    void camX; void camY; void z;
  }

  /** atmo（Game.updateWeather 同公式，Main.cs:62622） */

  constructor(key: string, x: number, y: number) {
    super();
    this.npcKey = key;
    this.x = x;
    this.y = y - this.h;
    const town = TOWN_NPC_IDS[key];
    this.vanillaId = town?.id ?? 22; // 未登记 key 兜底向导贴图
    this.givenName = newNpcName(this.vanillaId);
    const v = VANILLA_NPCS[String(this.vanillaId)];
    this.maxHp = this.hp = v?.lifeMax ?? 250;
  }

  /** 被陷阱弹幕等命中：dmg - defense*0.5（下限 1），8 tick 无敌帧；
   *  死亡即移除——"重生"由入驻轮承担（原版 UpdateTime_SpawnTownNPCs：不在场且
   *  条件满足 → 重新入驻，≈次日回归），无需独立重生系统 */
  hurt(dmg: number, kbx: number, _kby: number, game: GameHooks): boolean {
    if (this.iframes > 0 || this.dead) return false;
    const books = (game.world.flags.combatBookWasUsed ? 1 : 0) + (game.world.flags.combatBookVolumeTwoWasUsed ? 1 : 0);
    const def = (VANILLA_NPCS[String(this.vanillaId)]?.defense ?? 15) + books * 8; // NPC.cs:53419
    dmg = Math.max(1, Math.round(dmg - def * 0.5));
    this.hp -= dmg;
    this.iframes = 8;
    game.addDamageNumber(this.cx, this.y, dmg, false, '#FFD060');
    // 受击血粒子(原版 StrikeNPC 尘近似,与 Enemy.hurt 同规)
    game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#B01818', 5, 1.4, { life: 22, grav: 0.06 });
    this.vx += kbx * 0.6;
    if (this.hp <= 0) {
      this.dead = true;
      game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#C03030', 8, 2);
      game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#8A1818', 4, 1.2);
      game.playSfxFiles(['NPC_Killed_1'], 0.8);
    } else {
      game.playSfxFiles(['NPC_Hit_1'], 0.6);
    }
    return true;
  }

  /** 战斗书增益（NPC.cs:53419-53430：每本 lifeMax+250 / 伤害出力×0.8 / 移速+0.25 /
   *  防+8——伤害/移速两项无对应战斗系统，落 lifeMax/防；每本可叠加，由调用方按旗标次数调） */
  applyCombatBook(): void {
    this.maxHp += 250;
    this.hp = Math.min(this.maxHp, this.hp + 250);
  }

  private cbApplied = false;

  /** GetMagicAuraColor（NPC.cs:53317-53332）1:1：NPCID.Sets.MagicAuraColor
   *  （NPCID.cs:4853）五人恰好 = AttackType-2 魔法攻击族（54 裁缝/108 巫师/

	public void AddLight(int x, int y, Vector3 color)
	{
		_perFrameLights.Add(new PerFrameLight(new Point(x, y), color));
	}

	public void Clear()
	{
		_activeLightMap.Clear();
		_workingLightMap.Clear();
		_perFrameLights.Clear();
		_oldPerFrameLights.Clear();
	}

	public Vector3 GetColor(int x, int y)
	{
		if (!_activeProcessedArea.Contains(x, y))
		{
			return Vector3.Zero;
		}
		x -= _activeProcessedArea.X;
		y -= _activeProcessedArea.Y;

// 2D 网格贴图 NPC 帧引擎回归：657/576/577/696/564/565/668 七个"网格帧"NPC 的
// FindFrame 索引状态机（NPC.cs:67295+ 大 switch 专属 case）1:1 序列验证。
// 通用 vnpc 竖切路径会把网格表整行画出来（画错成多倍宽）——这些 NPC 的帧索引语义
// 必须与原版逐 tick 一致，切格绘制才有意义。
//
// 注意原版 frameCounter 语义：初值 0、每 tick 先 +=1 再比阈值——因此每段【首帧
// 比标称持帧少 1 tick】（如"每 4t 推进"实际首帧显示 3 tick）。以下断言全部按
// 反编译逐行手推的精确值书写。
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import {
  queenSlimeNextFrame,
  dd2OgreNextFrame,
  npc696NextFrame,
  darkMageNextFrame,
  deerclopsNextFrame,
  type NpcGridFrameState,
} from '../src/render/Renderer';

function st(): NpcGridFrameState { return { counter: 0, idx: 0 }; }

/** 驱动 n tick，收集每 tick 的帧索引 */
function run(fn: () => number, n: number): number[] {
  const out: number[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) out.push(fn());
  return out;
}
/** 状态序列去重压缩（保留顺序）——校验"持帧→推进"节奏用 */
function compress(seq: number[]): number[] {
  return seq.filter((v, i) => i === 0 || v !== seq[i - 1]);
}

describe('657 史莱姆皇后（FindFrame case 657，NPC.cs:67503-67614）', () => {
  it('一阶段上升：4→7 每 4 tick 推进，到 7 后悬停', () => {
    const s = st();
    const seq = run(() => queenSlimeNextFrame(s, { half: false, flying: false, vy: -5, ai0: 3, ai1: 0 }), 40);
    expect(compress(seq)).toEqual([4, 5, 6, 7]);
    expect(seq.slice(0, 3)).toEqual([4, 4, 4]); // 首帧 3 tick（counter 初值 0 先加后比）
  });
  it('二段(half)上升：4→7 后转 22，20→23 每 5 tick 循环', () => {
    const s = st();
    const seq = run(() => queenSlimeNextFrame(s, { half: true, flying: true, vy: 0, ai0: 0, ai1: 0 }), 60);
    expect(compress(seq).slice(0, 7)).toEqual([4, 5, 6, 22, 23, 20, 21]);
    // 23 → 回卷 20 继续循环（闭合）
    expect(compress(seq).slice(4, 8)).toEqual([23, 20, 21, 22]);
  });
  it('下降(vy>0)：8→10 每 8 tick 推进，停在 10', () => {
    const s = st();
    const seq = run(() => queenSlimeNextFrame(s, { half: false, flying: false, vy: 3, ai0: 0, ai1: 0 }), 60);
    expect(compress(seq)).toEqual([8, 9, 10]);
  });
  it('落地 ai0==5：帧 13/14/15 按 (ai1/3)%3', () => {
    for (const [ai1, want] of [[0, 13], [3, 14], [6, 15], [9, 13], [15, 15]] as const) {
      const s = st();
      expect(queenSlimeNextFrame(s, { half: false, flying: false, vy: 0, ai0: 5, ai1 })).toBe(want);
    }
  });
  it('落地 ai0==4：帧 12/11/10 按 ai1/15', () => {
    for (const [ai1, want] of [[0, 12], [15, 11], [30, 10], [45, 10], [60, 12]] as const) {
      const s = st();

 "new_string": "  /** num7 逐 type 水平微调（Main.cs:27010-27062；default 0） */\n  private static hatXOffset(id: number): number {\n    if (id === 550) return -4;\n    if (id === 588) return 0;\n    if (id === 227) return -4;\n    if (id === 228 || id === 678 || id === 681 || id === 682) return -2;\n    if ([17, 18, 19, 20, 22, 124, 229, 353, 633, 637, 638, 656, 670, 679, 680, 683, 684].includes(id)) return -1;\n    if ([37, 38, 54, 107, 108, 160, 207, 209].includes(id)) return -3;\n    if (id === 178 || id === 208 || id === 369) return 1;\n    return 0;\n  }\n\n  /** 城镇史莱姆集（NPCID.Sets.IsTownSlime NPCID.cs:4446：670/678-684；绑缚态\n   *  685/686 不在）——帽位 num5 取反 + 专属 num3/num4 档（Main.cs:26901-26928） */\n  private static readonly HAT_TOWN_SLIME = new Set([670, 678, 679, 680, 681, 682, 683, 684]);\n\n  /** num3 逐 type 档 + 帧修正（Main.cs:26845-26887：公主 663=1；宠物三档——\n   *  637 猫基准 6（帧 19/22-27 −2、11-15 +2）、638 狗 12、656 兔基准 6（帧 1-3 −2、\n   *  18-25 −4、8 −2）；城镇史莱姆（num5 另取反）：default 1、670/679 −1、681 −2、\n   *  682 −1(num4+2)、683 −1(num4−2)、684 −4）。帧号 = frame.Y/帧高。\n   *  返回 [num3, num4]。 */\n  static hatNum3(id: number, frame: number): [number, number] {\n    if (id === 663) return [1, 0];\n    if (id === 637) {\n      let v = 6;\n      if (frame === 19 || (frame >= 22 && frame <= 27)) v -= 2;   // 20/21 不在表内\n      else if (frame >= 11 && frame <= 15) v += 2;\n      return [v, 0];\n    }\n    if (id === 638) return [12, 0];\n    if (id === 656) {\n      let v = 6;\n      if (frame >= 1 && frame <= 3) v -= 2;\n      else if (frame >= 18 && frame <= 25) v -= 4;\n      else if (frame === 8) v -= 2;\n      return [v, 0];\n    }\n    if (Renderer.HAT_TOWN_SLIME.has(id)) {\n      if (id === 670 || id === 679) return [-1, 0];\n      if (id === 681) return [-2, 0];\n      if (id === 682) return [-1, 2];\n      if (id === 683) return [-1, -2];\n      if (id === 684) return [-4, 0];\n      return [1, 0];   // 678/680 与 default\n    }\n    return [0, 0];\n  }\n\n  /** 攻击/坐姿态 X 修正（Main.cs:27001-27008）：海盗 229 攻击态 ai[0]==12 → −4×dir；\n   *  酒保 550 坐姿 ai[0]==5 → +7×dir。仓内映射：229 = TownNPC.attackState 12\n   *  （TOWN_ATTACK_TYPE[229]=1 弓 → atkState 12，AI_007 :55902 同 ai[0] 值）；\n   *  550 坐姿未建模（ai0 恒 0）→ 分支值 1:1 备案待接 */\n  static hatStateOffsetX(id: number, ai0: number): number {\n    if (id === 229 && ai0 === 12) return -4;\n    if (id === 550 && ai0 === 5) return 7;\n    return 0;\n  }"
}

num6 = ... (ai[0]==5)

```csharp
Vector2 bottom = n.Bottom;

vector.X += num3 * num5;
vector.Y += array[num];

int num6 = 0;
if (n.ai[0] == 5f) { num6 = -4; ... }

if (n.type == 229 && n.ai[0] == 12f) vector.X -= num5 * 4;
if (n.type == 550 && n.ai[0] == 5f) vector.X += num5 * 7;

Utils.GetLerpValue default (not clamped param)... In vanilla `Utils.GetLerpValue(0f, 60f, num322)` — the default for `clamped` is... let me check Utils.GetLerpValue signature: `public static float GetLerpValue(float from, float to, float t, bool clamped = false)`. Hmm, actually in Terraria's Utils: `public static float GetLerpValue(float from, float to, float t, bool clamped = true)`. Let me check. The repo's glowLerpValue says "Utils.GetLerpValue 1:1 (Utils.cs:283-308, 含 from>to 反向钳位)" and doesn't clamp t to [0,1]! Looking at the repo impl:

```ts
private static glowLerpValue(from: number, to: number, t: number): number {
  if (from < to) { if (t < from) return 0; if (t > to) return 1; }
  else { if (t < to) return 1; if (t > from) return 0; }
  return (t - from) / (to - from);
}
```
This clamps. Fine — for lai0 in [0,60) it's the identity ramp anyway.

Wait — careful: for lai0<60, alpha15 = trunc((cos(lai0×2π/60)×0.5+0.5)×32). At lai0=60: cos(2π)=1 → (0.5+0.5)×32=32 → alpha15=32 → a = 32×0.75/255 ≈ 0.0941. And no lerp3 fade (lai0>=60 branch not taken). ✓

At lai0=30: cos(π)=−1 → (−0.5+0.5)×32 = 0 → alpha15=0 → a=0 ✓. scale = 1−0.5×(1−0.25) = 0.625 ✓. radius = 250 ✓.

At lai0=0.5: lerp3 = 0.5/60 = 0.008333; lerp3² = 6.94e-5; scale = 1−0.5×(1−6.94e-5) ≈ 0.50003. "toBeCloseTo(0.5, 6)" → |0.50003−0.5| = 3.5e-5 < 5e-7? No! toBeCloseTo(x, 6) requires |diff| < 0.5×10^-6 = 5e-7. 3.5e-5 > 5e-7 → FAILS. Let me use precision 4: 3.5e-5 < 5e-5 ✓. Or compute exactly. I'll write the exact expectation instead: `1 - 0.5 * (1 - (0.5 / 60) ** 2)`.

 "content": "// NPC 绘制侧登记遗留核销回归（2026-08-13 六件套，标杆 ../Terarria1456/Terraria/Main.cs）：\n//   ① 568/569 枯萎兽出生紫雾（:25899-25907）+ 死亡紫魂环 Extra_89×16 @400\n//      （:26130-26155）——参数纯函数 npcWitherBirthParams / npcSoulRingParams\n//   ② 城镇宠物 637/638/656 派对帽帧修正分支（drawPartyHat 主 :26849-26887）\n//   ③ 229 海盗（ai[0]==12 → −4）/ 550 酒保（ai[0]==5 → +7）帽位 X 修正（:27001-27008）\n//   ④ 160 松露人 / 209 电子人微光态 Glow 变体档（:25769/:25783，drawTownNPC 消费）\n//   ⑤ 月总死亡白闪层位（MoonlordDeathDrama.DrawWhite :61763 = 帧尾，\n//      ScreenDarkness 后 / ScreenObstruction 前）\n//   ⑥ gore 16/17 火苗点光注入（Gore.Update 尾段 :1078-1101 AddLight 语义；\n//      生产消费点 = Game.ts 光收集循环，与火把/发光族同惯例）\n// 只读静态参数/素材/源序，不依赖 DOM（vitest node 环境，同 visual-extras.test.ts）。\nimport { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';\nimport { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';\nimport { join } from 'node:path';\n// pngjs 无类型声明（同 tests/visual-extras.test.ts 的既有用法）\n// @ts-ignore\nimport { PNG } from 'pngjs';\nimport { Renderer, townSheetFrameCount } from '../src/render/Renderer';\nimport { TILE } from '../src/core/constants';\nimport { GorePiece, liveGorePieces, resetGorePool } from '../src/entities/GorePiece';\n\nconst SRC = join(__dirname, '..', 'src');\nconst VANILLA = join(__dirname, '..', 'public', 'sprites', 'vanilla');\nconst srcOf = (rel: string) => readFileSync(join(SRC, rel), 'utf8');\n/** PNG IHDR 宽高（偏移 16/20，同 visual-extras.pngSize） */\nconst pngSize = (f: string): [number, number] => {\n  const b = readFileSync(join(VANILLA, f));\n  return [b.readUInt32BE(16), b.readUInt32BE(20)];\n};\n\ndescribe('① 568/569 枯萎兽出生紫雾（Main.cs:25899-25907）', () => {\n  it('3 份、半径 8×num300 收敛（8→0）、加性强度 num315²（0→1）', () => {\n    expect(Renderer.npcWitherBirthParams(60)).toBeNull();   // 出生完成 → 无雾\n    expect(Renderer.npcWitherBirthParams(100)).toBeNull();\n    const p0 = Renderer.npcWitherBirthParams(0)!;\n    expect(p0.count).toBe(3);                               // num299=3\n    expect(p0.radius).toBeCloseTo(8, 9);                    // num301×num300 = 8×1\n    expect(p0.fade).toBe(0);                                // (0/60)²\n    const p30 = Renderer.npcWitherBirthParams(30)!;\n    expect(p30.radius).toBeCloseTo(8 * (1 - 0.25), 9);      // 8×(1−0.5²)=6\n    expect(p30.fade).toBeCloseTo(0.25, 9);\n    const p59 = Renderer.npcWitherBirthParams(59)!;\n    expect(p59.radius).toBeCloseTo(8 * (1 - (59 / 60) ** 2), 9);\n    expect(p59.fade).toBeCloseTo((59 / 60) ** 2, 9);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('① 568/569 死亡紫魂环（Main.cs:26130-26155）', () => {\n  it('份数恒 16 @半径 400×num323、π/750 慢旋（num324=lai0×0.0041887905）', () => {\n    expect(Renderer.npcSoulRingParams(0)).toBeNull();       // localAI[0]>0 门\n    expect(Renderer.npcSoulRingParams(-3)).toBeNull();\n    const p = Renderer.npcSoulRingParams(60)!;\n    expect(p.count).toBe(16);\n    expect(p.radius).toBeCloseTo(400, 6);                   // num323=1（≥60 无膨胀段）\n    expect(p.scale).toBe(1);\n    expect(p.angle0).toBeCloseTo(60 * 0.0041887905, 9);\n  });\n  it('A 通道 = trunc((cos(lai0·2π/60)·0.5+0.5)×32)×0.75/255（60t 脉动）', () => {\n    const p60 = Renderer.npcSoulRingParams(60)!;            // cos(2π)=1 → 32 满档\n    expect(p60.alpha).toBeCloseTo((32 * 0.75) / 255, 9);\n    const p30 = Renderer.npcSoulRingParams(30)!;            // cos(π)=−1 → 0\n    expect(p30.alpha).toBe(0);\n    const p15 = Renderer.npcSoulRingParams(15)!;            // cos(π/2)=0 → 16\n    expect(p15.alpha).toBeCloseTo((16 * 0.75) / 255 * 0.25, 9);  // 再 ×lerp(0,60,15)=0.25\n  });\n  it('lai0<60 段：×GetLerpValue(0,60) 渐入 + num323=Lerp(1,0.5,1−lerp²)（0.5→1 膨胀）', () => {\n    const q = Renderer.npcSoulRingParams(30)!;\n    expect(q.scale).toBeCloseTo(1 - 0.5 * (1 - 0.25), 9);   // 0.625\n    expect(q.radius).toBeCloseTo(400 * 0.625, 6);\n    const r = Renderer.npcSoulRingParams(0.5)!;\n    expect(r.scale).toBeCloseTo(1 - 0.5 * (1 - (0.5 / 60) ** 2), 9);\n    expect(r.alpha).toBeCloseTo(0, 4);                      // lerp≈0.0083 → 近零渐入\n  });\n  it('灵气态期间恒在环：localAI[0] 任意正数（AI_107 :63882 每 tick +1）都不关环', () => {\n    for (const t of [1, 7, 59, 61, 120, 999]) {\n      expect(Renderer.npcSoulRingParams(t)).not.toBeNull();\n    }\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('② 城镇宠物派对帽帧修正分支（Main.cs:26849-26887）', () => {\n  it('637 猫：基准 6；帧 19/22-27 → −2、11-15 → +2（20/21 是缺口帧不动）', () => {\n    expect(Renderer.hatNum3(637, 0)).toEqual([6, 0]);\n    expect(Renderer.hatNum3(637, 10)).toEqual([6, 0]);\n    expect(Renderer.hatNum3(637, 19)).toEqual([4, 0]);\n    expect(Renderer.hatNum3(637, 22)).toEqual([4, 0]);\n    expect(Renderer.hatNum3(637, 27)).toEqual([4, 0]);\n    expect(Renderer.hatNum3(637, 20)).toEqual([6, 0]);      // 缺口帧\n    expect(Renderer.hatNum3(637, 21)).toEqual([6, 0]);\n    expect(Renderer.hatNum3(637, 11)).toEqual([8, 0]);\n    expect(Renderer.hatNum3(637, 15)).toEqual([8, 0]);\n    expect(Renderer.hatNum3(637, 16)).toEqual([6, 0]);\n  });\n  it('638 狗恒 12（无帧修正）', () => {\n    for (const f of [0, 5, 14, 27]) expect(Renderer.hatNum3(638, f)).toEqual([12, 0]);\n  });\n  it('656 兔：基准 6；帧 1-3 → −2、18-25 → −4、8 → −2', () => {\n    expect(Renderer.hatNum3(656, 0)).toEqual([6, 0]);\n    expect(Renderer.hatNum3(656, 1)).toEqual([4, 0]);\n    expect(Renderer.hatNum3(656, 3)).toEqual([4, 0]);\n    expect(Renderer.hatNum3(656, 4)).toEqual([6, 0]);\n    expect(Renderer.hatNum3(656, 8)).toEqual([4, 0]);\n    expect(Renderer.hatNum3(656, 17)).toEqual([6, 0]);\n    expect(Renderer.hatNum3(656, 18)).toEqual([2, 0]);\n    expect(Renderer.hatNum3(656, 25)).toEqual([2, 0]);\n    expect(Renderer.hatNum3(656, 26)).toEqual([6, 0]);\n  });\n  it('663 公主 1 + 城镇史莱姆档（IsTownSlime NPCID.cs:4446，:26901-26928）', () => {\n    expect(Renderer.hatNum3(663, 0)).toEqual([1, 0]);\n    expect(Renderer.hatNum3(670, 0)).toEqual([-1, 0]);\n    expect(Renderer.hatNum3(679, 0)).toEqual([-1, 0]);\n    expect(Renderer.hatNum3(678, 0)).toEqual([1, 0]);\n    expect(Renderer.hatNum3(680, 0)).toEqual([1, 0]);\n    expect(Renderer.hatNum3(681, 0)).toEqual([-2, 0]);\n    expect(Renderer.hatNum3(682, 0)).toEqual([-1, 2]);\n    expect(Renderer.hatNum3(683, 0)).toEqual([-1, -2]);\n    expect(Renderer.hatNum3(684, 0)).toEqual([-4, 0]);\n    expect(Renderer.hatNum3(22, 0)).toEqual([0, 0]);        // 普通城镇 NPC 无档\n  });\n  it('宠物 NPCFramingGroup 补齐（NPCID.cs:4875：637→4 / 638→5 / 656→6 / 633→1）', () => {\n    const src = srcOf('render/Renderer.ts');\n    const tbl = src.slice(src.indexOf('HAT_FRAMING_GROUP'), src.indexOf('};', src.indexOf('HAT_FRAMING_GROUP')));\n    for (const pair of ['633: 1', '637: 4', '638: 5', '656: 6']) expect(tbl).toContain(pair);\n    // HAT_FRAME_GROUPS 组 4/5/6 长度 = 28/28/26（NPCID.cs:4879-4928）\n    const g = Renderer;\n    void g;\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('③ 229 海盗 / 550 酒保帽位 X 修正（Main.cs:27001-27008）', () => {\n  it('海盗弓态 ai[0]==12 → −4；酒保坐姿 ai[0]==5 → +7；其余 0', () => {\n    expect(Renderer.hatStateOffsetX(229, 12)).toBe(-4);\n    expect(Renderer.hatStateOffsetX(229, 0)).toBe(0);\n    expect(Renderer.hatStateOffsetX(229, 10)).toBe(0);\n    expect(Renderer.hatStateOffsetX(229, 14)).toBe(0);\n    expect(Renderer.hatStateOffsetX(550, 5)).toBe(7);\n    expect(Renderer.hatStateOffsetX(550, 0)).toBe(0);\n    expect(Renderer.hatStateOffsetX(550, 10)).toBe(0);\n    expect(Renderer.hatStateOffsetX(208, 12)).toBe(0);\n  });\n  it('仓内消费链：229 弓态经 TownNPC.attackState 喂入（TOWN_ATTACK_TYPE[229]=1 → 12）', () => {\n    const src = srcOf('entities/TownNPC.ts');\n    expect(src).toContain('get attackState(): number');\n    const r = srcOf('render/Renderer.ts');\n    expect(r).toContain(\"Renderer.hatStateOffsetX(n.vanillaId, n.attackState)\");\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('④ 160/209 微光态 Glow 变体档（Main.cs:25769/:25783）', () => {\n  it('档映射：160 微光态 Glow_352；209 微光态按 altTexture 选 350/351', () => {\n    expect(Renderer.npcGlowVariantTex(160, false, 0)).toBe('vanilla/Glow_166.png');\n    expect(Renderer.npcGlowVariantTex(160, true, 0)).toBe('vanilla/Glow_352.png');\n    expect(Renderer.npcGlowVariantTex(160, true, 1)).toBe('vanilla/Glow_352.png');   // 160 不看 altTexture\n    expect(Renderer.npcGlowVariantTex(209, false, 1)).toBe('vanilla/Glow_167.png');\n    expect(Renderer.npcGlowVariantTex(209, true, 0)).toBe('vanilla/Glow_350.png');\n    expect(Renderer.npcGlowVariantTex(209, true, 2)).toBe('vanilla/Glow_350.png');\n    expect(Renderer.npcGlowVariantTex(209, true, 1)).toBe('vanilla/Glow_351.png');\n    expect(Renderer.npcGlowVariantTex(108, false, 0)).toBeNull();                    // 其余城镇 NPC 无档\n  });\n  it('Glow 表与 NPC 表帧数同构（drawTownNPC 按帧高切片的前提）', () => {\n    for (const [tex, id] of [\n      ['Glow_166.png', 160], ['Glow_352.png', 160],\n      ['Glow_167.png', 209], ['Glow_350.png', 209], ['Glow_351.png', 209],\n    ] as const) {\n      const [, h] = pngSize(tex);\n      const count = townSheetFrameCount(id);\n      expect(h % count, `${tex} 高 ${h} 须整除 ${count} 帧`).toBe(0);\n    }\n    expect(townSheetFrameCount(160)).toBe(23);   // Glow_166/352 = 1380 = 23×60\n    expect(townSheetFrameCount(209)).toBe(26);   // Glow_167/350/351 = 1456 = 26×56\n  });\n  it('drawTownNPC 已挂叠画（'lighter' + (128−α/2)/255，α=城镇 NPC 静态 0）', () => {\n    const src = srcOf('render/Renderer.ts');\n    expect(src).toContain('Renderer.npcGlowVariantTex(n.vanillaId, n.shimmered, n.altTexture)');\n    expect(src).toContain(\"ctx.globalAlpha = 128 / 255;\");\n    expect(src).toContain(\"ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';\");\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('⑤ 月总死亡白闪层位（MoonlordDeathDrama.DrawWhite Main.cs:61763）', () => {\n  it('调用点 = 帧尾：实体绘制与光照合成之后、ScreenObstruction 之前', () => {\n    const src = srcOf('render/Renderer.ts');\n    const entities = src.indexOf('// 4. 实体');\n    const lightComp = src.indexOf('// 7. 光照合成');\n    const flash = src.indexOf('this.sky.drawMoonlordWhiteFlash(ctx, viewW, viewH);');\n    const obstruction = src.indexOf('this.updateAndDrawScreenObstruction(ctx, cam, viewW, viewH, player);');\n    expect(entities).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    expect(lightComp).toBeGreaterThan(entities);\n    expect(flash).toBeGreaterThan(lightComp);\n    expect(obstruction).toBeGreaterThan(flash);\n  });\n  it('2d' 世界变换段不再画白闪（原偏差位）', () => {\n    const sky = srcOf('render/SkyRenderer.ts');\n    const worldFx = sky.indexOf('drawWorldFx(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D');\n    const body = sky.slice(worldFx, sky.indexOf('}', sky.indexOf('void camX; void camY; void z;', worldFx)));\n    expect(body).not.toContain('drawMoonlordWhitening');\n    // 帧尾出口仍调本体（T 代理实装的白闪）\n    const tail = sky.indexOf('drawMoonlordWhiteFlash(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D');\n    expect(tail).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    expect(sky.slice(tail)).toContain('this.drawMoonlordWhitening(ctx, viewW, viewH);');\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('⑥ gore 16/17 火苗点光注入（Gore.Update 尾段 :1078-1101）', () => {\n  /** LightingEngine.addLight 的 mock 收集器（生产消费点 Game.ts 光收集循环） */\n  const makeSink = () => {\n    const calls: Array<[number, number, number, number, number]> = [];\n    return {\n      calls,\n      addLight: (x: number, y: number, r: number, g: number, b: number) => {\n        calls.push([x, y, r, g, b]);\n      },\n    };\n  };\n  it('gore active 且 light>0 → 注入 lightRGB 的 tx/ty/rgb（16 号橙火衰减通道）', () => {\n    resetGorePool();\n    const p = GorePiece.newGore(16, 10 * TILE, 5 * TILE, 0, 0, 1)!;\n    p.fixedUpdate(1, { world: null } as never);              // scale 0.7→0.69\n    const sink = makeSink();\n    const inBounds = () => true;\n    for (const gp of liveGorePieces()) {                     // 同 Game.ts 注入式\n      const l = gp.lightRGB();\n      if (l && inBounds(l.tx, l.ty)) sink.addLight(l.tx, l.ty, l.r, l.g, l.b);\n    }\n    expect(sink.calls.length).toBe(1);\n    const [tx, ty, r, g, b] = sink.calls[0];\n    expect(tx).toBe(Math.floor((p.x + (p.texW * p.scale) / 2) / 16));\n    expect(ty).toBe(Math.floor((p.y + (p.texH * p.scale) / 2) / 16));\n    expect(r).toBeCloseTo(p.scale, 6);                       // light×scale（1×0.69）\n    expect(g).toBeCloseTo(p.scale * 0.8, 6);                 // 16 号 G×0.8\n    expect(b).toBeCloseTo(p.scale * 0.3, 6);                 // 16 号 B×0.3\n    resetGorePool();\n  });\n  it('17 号蓝火通道 (R×0.3, G×0.6, B)；light==0 / dead / 越界 → 不注入', () => {\n    resetGorePool();\n    const q = GorePiece.newGore(17, 30 * TILE, 5 * TILE, 0, 0, 1)!;\n    const plain = GorePiece.newGore(4, 40 * TILE, 5 * TILE, 0, 0, 1)!;   // 非 16/17：light=0\n    expect(plain.light).toBe(0);\n    const sink = makeSink();\n    const inBounds = () => true;\n    for (const gp of liveGorePieces()) {\n      const l = gp.lightRGB();\n      if (l && inBounds(l.tx, l.ty)) sink.addLight(l.tx, l.ty, l.r, l.g, l.b);\n    }\n    expect(sink.calls.length).toBe(1);                        // 仅 17 号\n    expect(sink.calls[0][2]).toBeCloseTo(0.7 * 0.3, 4);\n    expect(sink.calls[0][3]).toBeCloseTo(0.7 * 0.6, 4);\n    expect(sink.calls[0][4]).toBeCloseTo(0.7, 4);\n    // 越界 → 界内门拦下（inBounds 守卫，同 Game.ts 表达式）\n    const sink2 = makeSink();\n    const never = () => false;\n    for (const gp of liveGorePieces()) {\n      const l = gp.lightRGB();\n      if (l && never(l.tx, l.ty)) sink2.addLight(l.tx, l.ty, l.r, l.g, l.b);\n    }\n    expect(sink2.calls.length).toBe(0);\n    // dead → lightRGB 恒 null（Gore.active 语义）\n    let ticks = 0;\n    while (!q.dead && ticks++ < 8000) q.fixedUpdate(1, { world: null } as never);\n    expect(q.dead).toBe(true);\n    expect(q.lightRGB()).toBeNull();\n    resetGorePool();\n  });\n});\n"
}

x < 48; x++) w.store.setTile(x, 40, 1);\n    return w;\n  };\n  it('gore active 且 light>0 → 注入 lightRGB 的 tx/ty/rgb（16 号橙火衰减通道）', () => {\n    resetGorePool();\n    const w = goreWorld();\n    const p = GorePiece.newGore(16, 10 * TILE, 5 * TILE, 0, 0, 1)!;\n    p.fixedUpdate(1, { world: w } as never);   // 非 16/17：light=0\n    expect(plain.light).toBe(0);\n    const sink = makeSink();\n    const inBounds = (x: number, y: number) => w.store.inBounds(x, y);\n    for (const gp of liveGorePieces()) {\n      const l = gp.lightRGB();\n      if (l && inBounds(l.tx, l.ty)) sink.addLight(l.tx, l.ty, l.r, l.g, l.b);\n    }\n    expect(sink.calls.length).toBe(1); import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';

const b = await puppeteer.launch({
  executablePath: '/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome',
  headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'],
});
const p = await b.newPage();
p.on('pageerror', e => console.log('ERR:', String(e).slice(0, 300)));
await p.goto('http://localhost:5199/?play=small', { waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded', timeout: 60000 });
await p.waitForFunction(() => window.__swGame && window.__swGame.liquid, { timeout: 600000 });
await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 2500));

const out = await p.evaluate(async () => {
  const g = window.__swGame;
  const { Lang } = await import('/src/i18n/Lang.ts');
  const { TownNPC } = await import('/src/entities/TownNPC.ts');
  const res = {};
  const list = () => g.entities.npcs.map((n) => ({ key: n.npcKey, bound: !!n.bound, home: !!n.home }));

  // ① 初始：向导 + 老人 + 5 名 bound 救援 NPC
  res.initial = list();

  // ② bound 采样对话与解救：造一个哥布林直接判条件 + 解救在场地牢机械师
  const goblin = g.entities.npcs.find((n) => n.npcKey === 'goblin_tinkerer');
  res.goblinBound = goblin ? !!goblin.bound : null;
  // 解救一名（直接调用私有方法模拟右键）
  if (goblin) { g.freeBoundNpc(goblin); }
  res.afterFree = {
    flag: g.world.flags.savedGoblin,
    bound: g.entities.npcs.find((n) => n.npcKey === 'goblin_tinkerer')?.bound,
  };
  // 解救后对话应走正式池(哥布林 121-136/126/309/Chatter)
  if (goblin) {
    const pool = new Set();
    ['121','122','123','124','125','126','127','128','129','130','131','132','133','134','135','136','309']
      .forEach((k) => pool.add(Lang.text('LegacyDialog.' + k)));
    pool.add(Lang.chatter('GoblinTinkererChatter'));
    let ok = true;
    for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) if (!pool.has(g.townNpcChat(goblin))) ok = false;
    res.goblinChat = ok;
  }

  // ③ 商店：商人股票来自 JSON（基础含火把 8/砧 35，夜晚荧光棒 282，困难模式治疗药水 188）
  const merch = new TownNPC('merchant', 0, 0);
  g.world.clock.timeOfDay = 0.5; // 白天
  const dayStock = g.shopStockFor(merch).map((e) => e[0]);
  g.world.clock.timeOfDay = 0.9; // 夜晚
  const nightStock = g.shopStockFor(merch).map((e) => e[0]);

```
export class EntityManager {
  nextId = 1;
  enemies: Entity[] = [];
  drops: Entity[] = [];
  npcs: Entity[] = [];
  projectiles: Entity[] = [];
  props: Entity[] = [];   // 场景物件（墓碑等不可拾取物理实体）
  critters: Entity[] = []; // 小动物（无害被动生物）

  add(e: Entity, bucket: 'enemies' | 'drops' | 'npcs' | 'projectiles' | 'props' | 'critters') {
    e.id = this.nextId++;
    this[bucket].push(e);
  }

  update(dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
    // 就地压缩替代 6×filter(2026-08 审计 G9:每 tick 6 个新数组,60Hz 恒定分配)
    for (const list of [this.enemies, this.drops, this.npcs, this.projectiles, this.props, this.critters]) {
      for (const e of list) e.fixedUpdate(dt, game);
      compact(list);
    }
  }

  clear() {
    this.enemies.length = 0;
    this.drops.length = 0;
    this.npcs.length = 0;
    this.projectiles.length = 0;
    this.props.length = 0;
    this.critters.length = 0;
  }

  all(): Entity[] {
    return [...this.enemies, ...this.drops, ...this.npcs, ...this.projectiles, ...this.props, ...this.critters];
  }
}

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * 一次性诊断脚本的统一 runner(防孤儿进程)。
 *
 * 背景:2026-08-10 发现 7 个 `vite-node tools/_diag-gen.mjs` 孤儿进程,
 * 每个占满 1 核空转 4 小时(脚本 import 链带起不可退出的活句柄,TERM 信号都无法处理)。
 * 约定:以后所有 _diag-*.mjs 一律经本 runner 运行,不再直接 npm exec vite-node。
 *
 * 用法:
 *   node tools/run-diag.mjs [--timeout=ms] <script.mjs> [传给脚本的参数...]
 *
 * 双层架构:
 *   - 子进程(--_child,内部):导入目标脚本;导入完成 0.5s 后强制 exit(0),
 *     不依赖事件循环自然排空——兜住"脚本主体完成但残留 interval/server 等活句柄"(本次事故形态);
 *   - 父进程(默认):只等子进程退出 + 看门狗;子进程超时(默认 120s)未退 → SIGKILL,exit 2。
 *     父进程事件循环始终空闲,因此子进程无论是同步死循环、永不 resolve 的 await
 *     还是任何未知卡死,都必然在 timeout 内被强杀。
 *
 * 退出码:0 正常 / 1 脚本异常或被信号终止 / 2 超时强杀 / 64 用法错误,其余透传脚本自设退出码。
 */
const DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS = 120_000;
const FLUSH_MS = 500;

// ---------- 子进程模式:导入目标脚本 ----------
if (argv[0] === '--_child') {
  const target = argv[1];
  try {
    const { pathToFileURL } = await import('node:url');

'http://localhost:5199';\nconst b = await puppeteer.launch({\n  executablePath: '/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome',\n  headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox', '--window-size=1280,800'],\n});\nconst page = await b.newPage();\nawait page.setViewport({ width: 1280, height: 800 });\nconst errors = [];\npage.on('pageerror', (e) => errors.push('PAGEERR: ' + String(e).slice(0, 300)));\npage.on('console', (m) => {\n  if (m.type() === 'error') errors.push('CONSOLE: ' + m.text().slice(0, 300));\n});\nawait page.goto(ORIGIN + '/?play=small', { waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded', timeout: 60000 });\nawait page.waitForFunction(() => window.__swGame && window.__swGame.liquid, { timeout: 600000 });\nawait new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 2500));\n\nconst res = await page.evaluate(async () => {\n  const g = window.__swGame;\n  const out = {};\n  const { TownNPC } = await import('/src/entities/TownNPC.ts');\n  const { Enemy } = await import('/src/entities/Enemy.ts');\n  const { toggleManualParty } = await import('/src/world/wiring/party.ts');\n  const { GorePiece } = await import('/src/entities/GorePiece.ts');\n  const dd2 = await import('/src/entities/bossAI_dd2.ts');\n\n  const p = g.player;\n  const px = Math.floor(p.cx / 16), py = Math.floor(p.cy / 16);\n  // 找脚下实心面\n  let floorY = py;\n  for (let y = py; y < py + 30; y++) {\n    if (g.world.store.isSolid(px, y)) { floorY = y; break; }\n  }\n  const spawnX = (dx) => (px + dx) * 16 + 8;\n  const groundY = (floorY - 1) * 16;\n\n  // ① 568 枯萎兽（出生紫雾 + 灵气环）——放在玩家旁 6 格，600 触发距内进灵气态\n  const beast = Enemy.fromVanilla(568, spawnX(6), groundY - 40);\n  out.beastSpawned = !!beast;\n  if (beast) g.entities.add(beast, 'enemies');\n\n  // ② 宠物 + ③ 海盗 + ④ 160/209（209 顺带 shimmered 翻 Glow_350 档）\n  const townKeys = ['town_cat', 'town_dog', 'town_bunny', 'pirate', 'truffle', 'cyborg'];\n  let i = 0;\n  for (const k of townKeys) {\n    const n = new TownNPC(k, spawnX(-8 - i * 4), groundY);\n    g.entities.add(n, 'npcs');\n    i++;\n  }\n  const truffle = g.entities.npcs.find((n) => n.npcKey === 'truffle');\n  const cyborg = g.entities.npcs.find((n) => n.npcKey === 'cyborg');\n  if (truffle) truffle.shimmered = true;      // Glow_352 档\n  if (cyborg) cyborg.shimmered = true;        // Glow_350 档（altTexture≠1）\n  out.townCount = g.entities.npcs.length;\n\n  // 派对帽：强制 ManualParty\n  toggleManualParty(g.partyState);\n  out.partyUp = !!g.renderer.scene?.partyUp;\n\n  // ⑥ gore 火苗点光\n  const before = g.lighting.perFrameCount;\n  const fire = GorePiece.newGore(16, spawnX(-2), groundY - 20, 0, -2, 1);\n  out.fireGore = !!fire;\n\n  // 跑 5s（≈300 tick）：出生 60t + 灵气态进入（windup 110t）+ 派对帽/帽子多帧\n  await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 5000));\n\n  out.lightPerFrame = g.lighting.perFrameCount;\n  out.lightBefore = before;\n  if (beast) {\n    const [lai0, lai3] = dd2.witherBeastLocal(beast);\n    out.beastLocal = { lai0, lai3, ai0: beast.ai0 };\n  }\n  out.sceneParty = !!g.renderer.scene?.partyUp;\n  out.pirateAtk = g.entities.npcs.find((n) => n.npcKey === 'pirate')?.attackState ?? null;\n  return out;\n});\n\n// 再等两帧后截图（覆盖 5 帧尾白闪调用点与 4.6 gore 层）\nawait new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1200));\nawait page.screenshot({ path: '/tmp/draw-leftovers-5201.png' });\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify(res, null, 2));\nconsole.log('ERRORS(' + errors.length + '):');\nfor (const e of errors.slice(0, 10)) console.log('  ' + e);\nawait b.close();\nprocess.exit(errors.length ? 1 : 0);\n"
}

{ count: prm.count, radius: Math.round(prm.radius), alpha: +prm.alpha.toFixed(4) } : null;\n    // 直调：包一层 drawImage 计数（世界 ctx 上的原语）\n    const r = g.renderer;\n    const orig = r.ctx.drawImage.bind(r.ctx);\n    let n = 0;\n    r.ctx.drawImage = (...a) => { n++; return orig(...a); };\n    try {\n      r.drawWitherSoulRing(beast);\n      out.ringDrawCalls = n;\n      out.ringThrew = false;\n    } catch (e) {\n      out.ringThrew = String(e).slice(0, 200);\n    } finally {\n      r.ctx.drawImage = orig;\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Glow 档直调（shimmered 松露人 + 电子人）\n  try {\n    for (const n of g.entities.npcs) g.renderer.drawTownNPC(n, g.world);\n    out.townGlowThrew = false;\n  } catch (e) {\n    out.townGlowThrew = String(e).slice(0, 200);\n  }\n  return out;\n});\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify(res, null, 2));\nconsole.log('ERRORS(' + errors.length + '):');\nfor (const e of errors.slice(0, 6)) console.log('  ' + e);\nawait b.close();\nprocess.exit(errors.length ?
